{"etymology_number":"1","lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"glosses":["Of, pertaining to, caused by, or characteristic of cowpox."],"tags":["historical","not-comparable"],"topics":["medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Of or pertaining to cowpox as a source of material for vaccination against smallpox; also, of or pertaining to such material used for vaccination."],"tags":["historical","not-comparable"],"topics":["immunology","medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Of, pertaining to, or derived from cattle."],"synonyms":[{"word":"bovine"}],"tags":["archaic","not-comparable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/vækˈsin/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈvækˌsin/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":266275,"upstream_offset":1330559040,"upstream_record_sha256":"4d57e74c2b8be1a04ac8b13ea4d1f076e6166f31c393100e4fa93a8782d18caf","word":"vaccine"}
{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"vaccines","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A substance given to stimulate a body's production of antibodies and provide immunity against a disease without causing the disease itself in the treatment, prepared from the agent that causes the disease (or a derivative of it; or a related, also effective, but safer disease), or a synthetic substitute; also, a dose of such a substance."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["immunology","medicine","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"My dog has had two vaccines this year.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The process of vaccination; immunization, inoculation."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["immunology","medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["The material taken from cowpox pustules used for vaccination against smallpox."],"tags":["countable","historical","uncountable"],"topics":["immunology","medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["The disease cowpox, especially as a source of material for vaccination against smallpox."],"tags":["also","countable","obsolete","uncountable"],"topics":["immunology","medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Something defensive or protective in nature, like a vaccine (sense 1.1)."],"tags":["countable","figuratively","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A software program which protects computers against, or detects and neutralizes, computer viruses and other types of malware; an antivirus."],"tags":["countable","figuratively","uncountable"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/vækˈsin/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈvækˌsin/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":266276,"upstream_offset":1330568747,"upstream_record_sha256":"c263de8da1c837e113134496db31ae9177b19c63b9f8a99573fe7b16cbf431c9","word":"vaccine"}
